  - The Crafting tab shows each craft's limit and what lifts it.
- **Beasts are built differently:** some kinds carry traits, listed on the battle screen, that call for other moves:
  - **Shelled** (boars, spiders, dragons, the Kunpeng): blades glance off and can't sever, but crushing blows crack it.
  - **Soul-warded** (the nine-headed serpent, the qilins, the thunder dragon): soul arts barely touch it.
  - **Elusive** (leopards, crows, the phoenix): single blows often miss unless it is stunned, bound or slowed, or the art sweeps the field.
  - **Qi-drinking hide** (apes, lions, the chaos qilin): qi arts do little.
  - **Regenerating** (pythons, the serpent, the phoenix): its wounds close every round unless it burns or withers.
- **Art creation:**
  - A trait the form doesn't have naturally no longer asks for more insight than your realm allows (Qi Condensation used to need 22 of a possible 20).
  - The Trait list marks which traits you can give the art now and what the rest need, and grades beyond your realm are marked.
- **Refining an art to a higher grade** no longer leaves it weaker: it keeps enough quality to stay a little stronger than before (a perfected grade-3 method used to drop from ×1.67 to ×1.54).
- **Ascended immortals:** immortals already in the Upper Realm no longer "ascend" again every year, so the Ascended Immortals list stops repeating names and every entry shows its year.
- **Foldable sections:** click a heading to fold it away (remembered between sessions): Sell, Trade goods and People here in the Location tab, and Recent news and Your lineage in Relations.
- **Battle screen:** each art now sits in its own box with its moves beside it, so a move is never mistaken for the next art's. Guard, Flee, range and items come first, so they stay in view however many arts you have.
- **Arts tab:** a search box, and arts grouped by kind under headings that fold away (as does Create an art). An over-wide row can no longer push the tab, and its scroll bar, past the panel.
- **Crafting tab:** search designs, show only those you can craft now, **Forget** a design (your sect's too), or **Edit** one: it opens in the inventor with its materials, to change and save as a new design.
- **Art descriptions keep up:** a refined art's description names its new grade, a demonic art's names its new trait, and "An Immortal-grade" reads right. Older saves are corrected on load.
- **Avatars:** the avatar menu explains where your count comes from (your highest realm, or your Soul path's own), and what the Soul path's next realm would give.

## 0.9.13 (2026-11-01)

- **Arrays no longer stack without end:** at any place, the strongest qi-gathering array works in full, the next at half, the next at a quarter, and so on. All of them together raise the place's qi to at most 2.5×. Before, every array added its full bonus, so a cave could reach 30× qi or more.
- **Arrays anywhere:** a formation disk can be set up wherever you stay (Inventory: **Set up here**), not only at your abode. Secret realms and your inner world are the exceptions.
  - At your sect's mountain or your clan's estate, it serves every member who stays there.
  - Anywhere else (a city, a family member's home, a wild valley), it serves you, your family and your disciples.
  - You can keep five arrays at one place. The Location tab lists the arrays at your current place, and you can take your own back up.
- **Your clan follows you up:** ascending to the Upper Realm still leaves your sect behind, but no longer your clan. You become its **ancestor above the clouds**: the side panel and Sect tab show it. Each ancestor above adds +3% to the clan's breakthroughs, and you can send a tenth of your spirit stones down to its treasury. Saves already in the Upper Realm find their clan by surname.

## 0.9.12 (2026-11-01)

- **Avatars cross worlds with you:** going through a world gate or tearing a rift no longer leaves your avatars behind. They share your soul, so they step out at your side in the new world. One that was guarding an abode, teaching or protecting disciples goes back to cultivating, since that work stayed in the old world.

## 0.9.11 (2026-11-01)

- **Gear with special properties:** forged gear now takes more than force from its materials, and a smith can quench the work with spirit herbs.
  - **Weapons:** Vitality makes a blade **drink blood** (heals you by part of the damage it deals), Hard ore makes it **armour-breaking**, and Spirit makes it **qi-siphoning** (each blow draws the foe's qi into you). These work with bare strikes and weapon and fist arts.
  - **Armour:** Sharp makes it **thorned** (close blows bite back), Vitality makes it **regenerate** (wounds mend every round of battle), and Essence makes it a **qi spring**.
  - **Accessories:** Essence speeds **cultivation** and Vitality regenerates.
  - Each property is weighed against your realm and capped across all your gear. Existing designs keep their old effects; invent new ones to get these.
- **Craft scriptures matter:**
  - They lend more skill: a third more than before, plus 4.
  - Mastered, they also attune works of their element (better quality at 25%), save materials (a 30% chance to give back one unit of the dearest material at 50%), and leave a **signature** at 75%: one more pill or talisman a batch, and gear, formations and puppets 15% stronger.
- **Crafting is slower:**
  - Finer works take longer: a Heaven-grade pill takes three months, a Heaven-grade formation six. The Crafting tab shows each design's time.
  - Fewer pills and talismans come out of a batch.
  - Gathering finds a little less, and markets sell materials in smaller lots.

## 0.9.10 (2026-11-01)

- **Tidier battle screen:** every combat art (Arts tab) and every pill, talisman, formation disk and puppet (Inventory tab) has a **Shown in battle / Hidden in battle** toggle. Hidden ones stay off the battle screen; a **Show N hidden** button there brings them back for that fight. Items are hidden by name, so new pills of the same kind stay hidden too.
- **Ascension:** companions below Deity Transformation no longer follow you into the Upper Realm, where they died. They stay in the world below when you ascend, and part ways with you there.

## 0.9.9 (2026-11-01)

- **Bloodlines:** tempering a bloodline founded over an older one no longer swaps its art element for your current deepest dao.

## 0.9.8 (2026-11-01)

- **Companions in fights:** a new **Companions** setting (side panel and Settings) controls whether they join your fights:
  - **Fight beside me:** they always join (the default).
  - **Only when safe for them:** they sit out any fight with a foe of a greater realm than their own.
  - **Stay out of fights:** they never join.

  It covers travelling companions, tamed beasts and sworn kin.
- **Subdue** is now a standing choice: once on, it stays on from fight to fight until you turn it off. It can also be set in Settings.

## 0.9.7 (2026-11-01)

- **Bloodlines keep their gifts:** a bloodline you founded keeps its own two attributes and its element for good. Tempering it or folding in beast blood used to re-pick them from whatever you had grown strongest in since. A body cultivator could lose the line's Agility and Comprehension (and the travel speed Agility brings), and its element could change. Older lines read their gifts back the first time they are reshaped. A reshape never lowers anything a line already gives.

## 0.9.6 (2026-11-01)

- **Qi pills nerfed:**
  - A qi pill is now worth a number of months of your own cultivation, doubling with each grade (about 5 months at grade 1, 18 at grade 2, 33 at grade 3 and 90 at grade 4). Before, it restored a share of a whole stage, so one pill could replace decades of cultivation.
  - Each further qi pill in the same minor stage works a quarter less, and the count starts over at your next stage.
  - Secret-realm spirit pools now give two years of your own cultivation instead of 40% of a stage.

## 0.9.5 (2026-11-01)

- **Founding your own bloodline:**
  - Whatever bloodline you already carry is kept in full: attributes, element arts, healing, lifespan and travel speed, at the purity you held. It used to be kept only at 60%+ purity, and then at half strength.
  - Body-path cultivators can found a bloodline from Foundation Building (everyone else from Nascent Soul).

## 0.9.4 (2026-11-01)

- **Art moves:** every art opens more moves as you master it. In battle they appear as ↳ buttons beside the art; the Arts tab lists them.
  - **Attack arts** strike with their own trait at first. At 15% mastery they learn **Full Force**; at 30% and 60%, two more traits their form allows (a sword may pierce or sever, a fist stun or shatter).
  - **Defense arts:** Barrier, then **Counter Stance** at 25% (answers the next close blow), then **Qi Shield** at 50%.
  - **Movement arts:** Blur, then **Step In** and **Step Away** at 25% (change range at once), then **Afterimage** at 50% (the next blow misses outright).
- **Full Force:** a blow that takes a round to gather and lands at 2.2× force, and everyone sees it coming. A barrier halves it, an afterimage dodges it, and a stun or seal scatters it. Foes use it too, and raise barriers or dodge when you gather one.
- **Defense and movement arts matter:** barriers (+50-65% protection) and blur (about +30% evasion) are now strong enough to be worth a turn. Equal fighters carrying both win about 60% of duels, against 45% without.
- **More than one dao companion:**
  - Propose again, or accept a suitor, while already bound. It needs more regard, and the arrogant refuse to be anyone's second.
  - Every companion you already have is bitterly hurt (−40 regard) and sours more each year they share you. An arrogant companion soured far enough may leave you, and anyone pushed far below zero will.
  - You can end a bond yourself from Interact, and each companion has their own "spend a year together" and dual cultivation buttons.
- **Secret realms close behind you:** one delve per opening. Once you leave, the realm seals until its next opening.
- **Guide search:** a search box filters the topics and lists every matching paragraph, with the matches highlighted.
- **Pills explained:**
  - Every pill in the inventory has a **For you** block saying what it would do for you: its effects as they would land, and how grade, element, repeated doses and toxins shape them. The same text shows when you hover a pill in battle.
  - Healing pills say "heals every wound by N", and a pill names its element once.
- **Integrity** (each body part's health, 0-100) is explained in the guide's "Your stats" section.
- **Fixes:** the battle screen fits a 720p window, and the Arts tab no longer runs off the edge of its panel.

## 0.9.3 (2026-10-31)

- **Wandering experts** give pointers once a year each, whether they teach you or turn you away. Approaching one cost no time, so you could ask again and again for endless insight.

## 0.9.2 (2026-10-31)

- **Sect intake seasons:** sects take new disciples for three months every few years, at a time of their own. The Sect tab, the map and the Calendar say when each gate opens next; aloof sects never announce it. The sect you choose at the start, Golden Core applicants and clans (for their own blood) take you at any time.
- **Sect libraries:** a sect that conquers another keeps only the arts that suit its own element, plus the three finest. Libraries no longer end up with more arts of other elements than their own.
- **Manuals** show the comprehension they need against what you bring, in the inventory, the market, the sect treasury and the sect library.
- **The Lantern Festival** really makes a friend now. It used to add too little regard to count as a friendship.
- **Fix:** the version number in the game now matches the build.

## 0.9.1 (2026-10-31)

- **Menus no longer stack:** spamming a key can no longer open the same menu again and again.
- **Hover boxes** on the Character tab explain attributes, foundation, breakthrough aid, cultivation speed, fame, infamy, karma, dao heart and body stats.
- **More clans:** clans and sects keep separate room (18 sects, 9 clans). Ambitious clans are founded far more often when there are few, and thriving clans send cadet branches to other regions.
- **Starting sect:** the sect you choose at the start always takes you (you carry a letter of recommendation), even when it is full or your root is below its standard.
- **Recruiting shown from afar:** open places and root standards, or why a sect would refuse you, show in the Sect tab, on the map and in the start picker.
- **Map** place, region and faction colours are more distinct.
- **Fix:** Crafting's profession buttons no longer run off the panel.
